avarkaḷatu ceyaṟkariya ceyali aṭikkaṭi ceytittāḷkaḷiṉ vāyilāka aṟinta piṟa ūrkaḷil irunta perumakkaḷ tattam ūrkaḷukku varumpaṭi avarkaḷukku aḻippu viṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷ avvaḻippiṉpaṭi ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum ceṉṟaṉar; āṅkāṅku tam tiṭṭatti viḷakkip pēciṉar. maṇivaṇṇaṉ, maṅki ākiya iruvaruṭiya coṟpoḻivukaḷ potumakkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkaḷik kavarntaṉa.
kumpakōṇattil kuñcitapātam ceṭṭiyār perum celvar. avar ōr aṟaniliyattit tōṟṟuvittār. ivvāṟē ovvoru periya nakarattilum aṟaniliyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭutta avvavvūr naṉmakkaḷ muyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, tam cukapōkaṅ kaḷik kuṟittuk koṇṭu ēḻikaḷ vāḻap poruḷutavi ceyyac celvar palar iṉṉum muṉvaravilli. eṉavē, cila ūrkaḷil maṭṭumē ciṟanta muṟiyil aṟaniliyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa.

inta niliyil caṭṭa maṉṟat tērtal neruṅkiyatu. “namatu tiṭṭam nāṭṭil niṟivēṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, nammavar caṭṭa maṉṟattil iṭam peṟutal vēṇṭum. eṉavē, māmā tērtalil niṟpatu nallatu” eṉṟu maṇivaṇṇaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. “eṉakkup patil maṅki niṟpatutāṉ nallatu” eṉṟār muttappar.
ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭirunta atē camayattil aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷattilum itē eṇṇam tōṉṟiyatu. avar avvūrp perumakkaḷ nālvarōṭu vantu maṅkiyik kaṇṭār; tām karutiyatit terivittār.
appōtu maṅki avari aṉpōṭu nōkki, “aṭikaḷē, ippoḻutu namatu nāṭṭil oruvar tāmē niṉṟu, `nāṉ tērtalukku niṟkiṟēṉ. eṉakku vākkaḷiyuṅkaḷ. nāṉpōy atic ceykiṟēṉ, itic ceykiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟum muṟiyi nāṉ aṟavē veṟukkiṟēṉ. mekkālē coṉṉatupōla, oru tokuti makkaḷ tammuḷ tāmē kūṭit taṅkaḷ nampikkikku uriya oruvarit tērtalil niṟutta vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam ovvoru tokutiyilum muṉkūṭṭip potumakkaḷē oruvarit tērnteṭuttu viṭṭāl, vēṟu palarum tērtalil niṉṟu poruḷi iḻakkamāṭṭār. palar poruḷum vīṇākātu. aracāṅkattiṟkum tērtal celavu mikak kuṟiyum. inta muṟi eṉṟu varumō, aṉṟutāṉ inta nāṭu uruppeṟum.
entak kaṭciyic cērntiruntālum, makkaḷukkup paṇattik koṭuttuttāṉ tērtalukku niṟpavar vākkup peṟukiṟār. mikac cila iṭaṅkaḷiltāṉ vākkāḷar, tērtalukku niṟpavariṉ collāṟ ṟaliyum ceyalāṟṟaliyum kaṇṭu vākkaḷikkiṉṟaṉar. aṭikaḷē, tiruppōrūriliruntu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu variyilum uḷḷa nilapparappu oru tokuti yākum. it tokutiyil oruvar tērnteṭukkappaṭa vēṇṭum. ittokuti makkaḷ taṅkaḷ cārpil nāṉ uṟuppiṉarākac cellavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpuvarāyiṉ, nāṉ cellat taṭiyilli. tāṅkaḷ yōcittu muṭivu kūṟuṅkaḷ” eṉṟāḷ.
nāṭṭil evvāṟu tērtal naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpati avaḷ vāyilākak kēṭṭa aṭikaḷ viyappuṟṟu niṉṟār; avaḷatu āḻnta akaṉṟa araciyal aṟivip pārāṭṭiṉār. “ammā, eṉ aṟivil ituvariyilum inta muṟi taṭṭuppaṭavilli. uṇmiyil itumātiritāṉ tērtal naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum. itaṉi ippōtē ceyalil ceyvōm. nāṉē itaṟkuriya ēṟpāṭṭic ceyvēṉ. ēḻi makkaḷ tuyar nīṅka vēṇṭumāyiṉ nīṅkaḷ caṭṭamaṉṟam cellattāṉ vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟi viṭipeṟṟār.
avar nērē pāṇṭiyaṉiṭam ceṉṟār; maṅkiyiṉ karutti aṟivittār. avaṉiyum centāmariyiyum aḻittukkoṇṭu kuḷattūr ceṉṟaṉar; kaṇṇaṉiyum tēṉmoḻiyiyum aḻittuk koṇṭu, at tokuti yiluḷḷa ovvōr ūrukkum ceṉṟaṉar. ovvōr ūrilum potukkūṭṭam kūṭṭiṉar; maṅkiyit tērnteṭuppatāl varum nalaṉkaḷi eṭuttu viḷakkiṉar.
piṉpu ovvōr ūrārum ūrkkūṭṭam kūṭṭiṉar; naṉku yōcit taṉar; piṟarkkut toṇṭu ceyvatiyē piṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkka mākak koṇṭa maṅkiyarkkaraciyiyē caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarāka niṟkumāṟu vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. ivvāṟu attokuti makkaḷ aṉivarum muṭivu ceytu vēṇṭukōḷ kaṭitaṅkaḷi maṅkikku aṉuppiṉar. aṭikaḷ mutaliya potunalat toṇṭarkaḷum palavūrp perumakkaḷum orunāḷ aṟaniliyattiṟku vantaṉar; maṅkiyi vaṟpuṟuttic caṭṭamaṉṟattiṟku uṟuppiṉarāka niṟkumāṟu ceytaṉar.
tērtal nāḷ vantatu. ovvoru tokutiyilum palar pōṭṭi yiṭṭaṉar; paṇatti vāri iṟittaṉar. āṉāl tiruppōrūrt tokutiyil ittakiya paṇac celavu illi; evarum tērtalil pōṭṭiyiṭavum illi. icceyti ceytittāḷkaḷil veḷiyā yiṟṟu. nāṭṭu naṉmakkaḷ ipputiya muṟiyi viyantu pārāṭṭiṉar.

makkaḷ nalak kaṭciyiṉarē perumpālarākat tērnteṭukkap peṟṟaṉar. akkaṭciyiṉ talivarāṉa vaṇaṅkāmuṭi amiccar aviyi amikkat toṭaṅkiṉār. avarum muṉṉāḷ payirttoḻil amiccar parantāmaṉārum aṟaniliyattiṟku vantaṉar. maṅki entak kaṭciyiyum cērātavaḷāka iruntālum, avaḷ ceytu vanta toṇṭiṉip pārāṭṭip payirt toḻil tuṟikku avaḷi amiccarāka amartta virumpuvatākavum, avaḷ taṉ paṇṇiyil ceyta cīrtiruttattiyē caṭṭa vāyilāka nāṭu muḻutum ceyyalāmeṉṟum, makkaḷ nalam oṉṟiyē karutupavaḷ ātalāl avaḷatu ceyalkaḷil tām taliyiṭuvatilli eṉṟum vaṇaṅkāmuṭi kūṟi, amiccar pataviyi ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉār. muttapparum aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷum maṇivaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōrum avaḷi vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. aṉivar vēṇṭukōḷmītu avaḷ patavi ēṟka oppukkoṇṭāḷ. 
mutaḷnāḷ naṭipeṟṟa caṭṭamaṉṟak kūṭṭattil maṅki eḻuntu niṉṟāḷ. caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar aṉivarum- etirkkaṭciyiṉar uḷpaṭa- kitaṭṭi āravāram ceytaṉar. etirkkaṭcit talivar ēkāmparam eḻuntu maṅkiyiṉ potunalat toṇṭip pārāṭṭip pēci avaḷōṭu tam kaṭciyiṉar ottuḻikkat tayār eṉpati makiḻcciyōṭu terivittār. vaṇaṅkāmuṭi mutaliya makkaḷ nalakkaṭcit talivarkaḷum avaḷ varavip pārāṭṭip pēciṉar; avaḷ tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa putu muṟiyi mika viyantu pārāṭṭiṉar.
“amiccar perumakkaḷē, caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷē, eṉṉip pārāṭṭi vāḻttuk kūṟiya uṅkaḷ aṉivarkkum eṉatu paṇivārnta vaṇakkam uriyatākum. eṉ tokuti makkaḷ viruppattiṉpaṭi nāṉ uṟuppiṉaḷ āṉēṉ; mutal amiccar pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ vaṟpuṟuttalālum, potu makkaḷukku nalam puriyalām eṉṟa nampikkiyālumē amiccar pataviyi ēṟṟuk koṇṭē.ṉ. eṅkaḷ paṇṇiyil nilakkiḻār- paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ oppantam ceytukoṇṭu irutiṟattārum oṉṟupaṭṭa uḷattarāyp payirt toḻili naṭattum muṟiyi nīṅkaḷ naṉku aṟivīrkaḷ. eṅkaḷ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi vēṟu cila paṇṇikaḷum iyaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. inta muṟiyip perumakkaḷākiya nīṅkaḷ ārāyuṅkaḷ; vēṇṭum tiruttaṅkaḷic ceyyuṅkaḷ.
itaṟkiiṭayil yāṉ ūrtōṟum cuṟṟi, ittiṭṭattiṟkup paṇṇiyārkaḷiṉ ātaraviyum paṇṇiyāṭkaḷiṉ ātaraviyum tiraṭṭuvēṉ. ivviru tiṟattāriṉ ātaraviyum peṟṟapiṟakē ittiṭṭam paṟṟiya caṭṭam im maṉṟattil ceyyappeṟum. vāḻap piṟanta makkaḷuṭiya maṉattip pakkuvappaṭuttiya piṟakē- avarkaḷ itu nāṭṭukku nallatu eṉpati uṇarntu icivu terivitta piṟakē ittiṭṭam caṭṭamātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu paṇivāṉa karuttu. tāṉē kaṉiyāta paḻattit taṭikoṇṭu aṭittuk kaṉiyavikka nāṉ virumpavilli.”
maṅkiyiṉ poruḷ niṟinta ippēccik kēṭṭa aviyōr akam mika makiḻntaṉar. etirkkaṭcit talivar eḻuntu, “uṅkaḷ karutti muḻumaṉattuṭaṉ etirkkaṭciyiṉar ātarikkiṉṟaṉar. uṅkaḷ tiṭṭattiṟku eṅkaḷ maṉamārnta vāḻttukkaḷit terivikkiṟōm.” eṉṟu makiḻcciyōṭu kūṟi amarntaṉar.
mutalamiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi eḻuntu, “taṉṉalamaṟṟa amiccar maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ karutti aracāṅkam ēṟṟukkoḷkiṟatu. pārāṭṭattakum ivvariya tiṭṭatti nilakkiḻārkaḷ aṉivarum paranta maṉappāṉmiyōṭum irakka uṇarcciyōṭum nāṭṭiṉ nalattil karuttuṭaṉum ārāyntu ēṟṟukkoḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ. ivvariya tiṭṭattik koṇṭu vantuḷḷa amiccarkku aracāṅkam taṉ muḻu ātaraviyum koṭukkiṉṟatu eṉpati mīṇṭum makiḻcciyōṭu terivittukkoḷkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu akamakiḻcci yuṭaṉ pēci muṭittār.
maṅkiyarkkaraciyip pārkkavēṇṭum- avaḷatu pēccik kēṭkavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpip pala ūrkaḷiliruntu perumakkaḷ palar caṭṭamaṉṟap pārviyāḷarāka vantu iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ aṉivarum maṅkiyiṉ eḷiya tūya uṭikaḷiyum aṉpu tavaḻum mukattiyum amitiyāṉa iṉiya pēcciyum kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aḷavaṟṟa makiḻcci aṭintaṉar; taṉakkeṉa vāḻāta ammātaraciyiṉ virinta cintiyiyum paranta nōkkattiyum eṇṇi eṇṇi viyantaṉar.
amiccar maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ putiya tiṭṭam avaḷatu caṭṭamaṉṟap pēccōṭum paṭattōṭum ellāc ceytit tāḷkaḷilum veḷivantaṉa. ellāk kaṭcic ceytit tāḷkaḷum avaḷatu tiṭṭattip palapaṭap pārāṭṭiṉa; “nāṭṭukku oru naṅki”, “mātaruḷ māṇikkam”, “taṉṉalamaṟṟa tūyavaḷ” eṉṟu avaḷukkup paṭṭaṅkoṭuttu makiḻntaṉa.
ovvōr ūrilum potumakkaḷuḷ nāṭṭu nalattilē karuttuṭiyavar- kaṟṟaṟinta periyōr eṉap palatiṟappaṭṭavar potukkūṭṭaṅkaḷik kūṭṭiṉar; maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭatti viḷakkip pēciṉar; potumakkaḷiṉ ātaravit tēṭiṉar; paṇṇiyārkaḷiṉ ottuḻippiyum vēṇṭiṉar. nāṭeṅkum ‘paṇṇiyār- paṇṇiyāḷ’ tiṭṭam paṟṟiyē pēccu naṭantatu. kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ peyar avaratu taṉṉalamaṟṟa toṇṭiṉāl evvāṟu mūlimuṭukkukaḷil ellām makkaḷāl uccarikkappaṭṭatō, avvāṟē maṅkiyark karaciyiṉ peyarum ittiṭṭattāl tamiḻakam muḻutum paraviyatu.

35. ‘maṅkiyarkkut taṉi araci’
maṅkiyarkkaraci amiccar patavi ēṟṟu iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa. kaḻinta iraṇṭu āṇṭuk kālattil avaḷ tamiḻakap pērūrkaḷiyum ciṟṟūrkaḷiyum pārvi yiṭṭāḷ; āṅkāṅku irunta perunilakkiḻārkaḷic cantittup pēciṉāḷ. nilat toṭarpāṉa aracāṅka aluvalarkaḷi aṅkaṅkē cantittut taṉ tiṭṭatti makkaḷiṭamum paṇṇiyārkaḷiṭamum eṭuttu viḷakkumpaṭi vēṇṭikkoṇṭāḷ.
ovvoru māvaṭṭattilum nilakkiḻār kūṭṭam kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. maṅki ovvoru kūṭṭattilum taṉ tiṭṭatti eṭuttu viḷakkiṉāḷ. pāṇṭiyaṉum nakkīraṉum avaḷ cārpil pala kūṭṭaṅkaḷil pēciṉar. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākac cuyanalam oṉṟilēyē ūṟi ūṟi vaḷarntu vanta nilakkiḻārkaḷ eḷitil icivarā? avaruḷ ōraḷavu naṭuvunili nōkkum paranta maṉappāṉmiyum koṇṭavar cilarē. avarkaḷ maṉamuvantu avaḷatu tiṭṭatti ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar; caṭṭam varuvataṟku muṉṉarē attiṭṭattic ceyaṟpaṭuttavum muyaṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu ovvōr māvaṭṭattilum nūṟṟukku irupattintu nilakkiḻār vītam maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭattic ceyaṟpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar. eñciya eḻupattintu pērum paṇṇiyāṭkaḷi ōraḷavu aṉpāka naṭattat toṭaṅkiṉar; ciṟitaḷavu kūliyum kūṭṭikkoṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷ maṉam inta aḷavukkuttāṉ iṭam koṭuttatu. āyiṉum, amiccar maṅkiyarkkaraci maṉam taḷaravilli. avaḷ mukamalarcciyōṭu ap paṇṇiyārkaḷi aṭikkaṭi pārttu, nalla muṟiyil avartam uḷḷaṅkaḷi māṟṟa muyaṉṟāḷ. mutal amiccar uḷḷiṭṭa piṟa amiccarkaḷum etirkkaṭciyiṉarum tattam pakutiyil it tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu tēṭiṉar. ivvāṟu amiccarkaḷum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum eṭuttukkoṇṭa iṭiviṭā muyaṟciyāl nūṟṟukku muppatupēr ōrāṇṭiṟkuḷ maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭatti ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam paṭippaṭiyāka maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭam nāṭṭil ceyaṟ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. nāṉkām āṇṭil maṅki miṉṉal vēkattil tamiḻakam muḻutum cuṟṟiṉāḷ. muttappar, aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ, maṇi vaṇṇaṉ, nakkīraṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya āṭavarum, centāmari, tēṉmoḻi, tāmarik kaṇṇi mutaliya peṇmaṇikaḷum pala ūrkaḷukkum ceṉṟu maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu tēṭiṉar. avarkaḷ ceṉṟa iṭantōṟum potumakkaḷ ātaravu mikutiyākak kiṭittatu.
“it tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu taravilliyāyiṉ, paṇṇiyil vēli ceyyamāṭṭōm” eṉṟu pala ūrkaḷil paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ kiḷarcci ceyyalāyiṉar. maṅki avviṭaṅkaḷukku virintāḷ; “paṇṇic cakōtararkaḷē, nīṅkaḷ ituvariyil paṇṇiyil vēli ceytu vantatatippōlavē iṉiyum vēli ceytu varavēṇṭum. nīṅkaḷ vēli ceyya maṟuttāl, nāṭṭu viḷiccal kuṟiyum. ataṉāl paṇṇiyār tuṉpappaṭamāṭṭār; uṇavu kiṭikkāmal potu makkaḷtām tuṉpappaṭuvar; nīṅkaḷum tuṉpappaṭa nēriṭum. uṅkaḷ kaṭamiyi nīṅkaḷ ceytu koṇṭu iruṅkaḷ. ittiṭṭattiṟku iṇaṅkumpaṭi paṇṇiyāri vēṇṭikkoḷḷuvatu eṅkaḷ kaṭami. eṉ colli mīṟi nīṅkaḷ vēli niṟuttam ceytāl, nāṉ amiccar pataviyiliruntu vilakiviṭuvēṉ. nāṉ uṅkaḷukkākavē ippataviyil irukkiṟēṉ eṉpati maṟantuviṭātīrkaḷ.”
paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ avaḷatu aṟivuriyi ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar; vaḻakkampōl paṇṇi vēlikaḷic ceyyalāyiṉar.
kiḷarcci ceytu vēli niṟuttam ceyyat toṭaṅkiya paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ aṟivuriyik kēṭṭup peṭṭippāmpukaḷ pōl aṭaṅki oḻuṅkāka vēli ceyyat toṭaṅkiyatik kaṇṭa paṇṇiyār cilar maṉam makiḻntaṉar; nallaṟivu peṟṟaṉar; putiya tiṭṭatti ēṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam maṅkiyiṉ iṭiviṭā uḻippāl avaḷ patavi ēṟṟa nāṉkām āṇṭiṉ iṟutikkuḷ nūṟṟukku eḻupattintu paṇṇiyār putiya tiṭṭattic ceyal paṭuttalāyiṉar. eñciya irupattintu pēr viṭākkaṇṭarāka iruntaṉar. āyiṉum, karumattilēyē kaṇṇāyirunta maṅkiyarkkaraci vāḷā irukkavilli; muttappar, aṭikaḷ, maṇivaṇṇaṉ, nakkīraṉ ākiya nālvariyum appaṇṇi yārkaḷiṉ maṉappōkki māṟṟa aṉuppiṉāḷ.
aracāṅka nilavari atikārikaḷuḷ perumpālōr nilakkiḻār allar. eṉavē, avar aṉivarum maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭatti maṉamāra ātarittaṉar; taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vaṭṭaṅkaḷilum māvaṭṭaṅ kaḷilum irunta paṇṇiyār kaḷuṭaṉ pēci, avartam uḷḷaṅ kaḷi māṟṟa muyaṉṟaṉar; ēḻikaḷiṉ pārāṭṭutalukku uriya varākumpaṭi aṟivuruttiṉar. ittakiya palamāṉa muyaṟciyiṉāl mēlum cilar attiṭṭattic ceyaṟpaṭutta muṉintaṉar.
nāṭu muḻuvatilum kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttatil, nūṟṟukkut toṇṇūṟu nilakkiḻār vītam putiya tiṭṭattic ceyaṟpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar eṉṟa uṇmi terintatu. itaṟkuḷ maṅki pataviyēṟṟu nāṉku āṇṭukaḷum oṉpatu mātaṅkaḷum muṭintuviṭṭaṉa. nūṟṟukkup pattuppērē nāṭu veṟukkum paṇpaṟṟavarāka iruntaṉar.
appattuppēriyum icivitta piṟakē tiṭṭam paṟṟiya caṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu maṅki eṇṇiṉāḷ. “ali ōynta piṟaku kaṭalil nīrāṭavēṇṭum’ eṉṟu niṉittāl- ali ōyappōvatum illi; nām nīrāṭappōvatum illi. eñciya pattuppērum oppuk koṇṭa piṟakē caṭṭam ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittāl avarkaḷ iciya mēlum pala varuṭaṅkaḷākalām. iṉṉum mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷil putiya tērtal varukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, ataṟkuḷ itaṉic caṭṭamākkuvatu nallatu. ipputiya tiṭṭam caṭṭamākkappaṭiṉ, aṭutta muṟiyum nāmē aracāṅkatti iyakka nērum. appoḻutu mēlum pala nalla tiṭṭaṅkaḷi uruvākkalām” eṉṟu mutalamiccar moḻintār.
maṅki avaratu yōcaṉiyi ēṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ; etirkkaṭcit talivaric cantittu avar yōcaṉiyik kēṭṭāḷ. mutalamiccar kūṟiyatiyē avarum kūṟiṉār. putiya tiṭṭam caṭṭacapiyil paṭikkappaṭṭuc caṭṭamākkappaṭum eṉṟa ceyti aracāṅka aṟikkiyilum ceytittāḷkaḷilum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. potumakkaḷ aḷavaṟṟa makiḻcci aṭintaṉar.
caṭṭacapiyil putiya tiṭṭam maṅkiyarkkaraciyāl paṭittu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. pala kaṭci uṟuppiṉarum pala aiyaṅkaḷik kēṭṭu teḷintaṉar. piṉṉar ellā uṟuppiṉar opputal mītu attiṭṭam caṭṭamākkappaṭṭatu. mutal amiccarum etirkaṭcit talivarum amiccar maṅkiyark karaciyiyum avaḷ koṇṭuvanta tiṭṭattiyum mikavum pārāṭṭip pēciṉar.
“amiccar perumakkaḷē, etirkaṭcit talivarē, maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷē, nāṉ oru tiṭṭattik koṇṭu vantēṉ. āṉāl ataṟkuc caṭṭamaṉṟattilum veḷi ulakilum muḻu ātaraviyum koṭutta perumakkaḷ nīṅkaḷē. uṅkaḷ ottuḻippu illi yāyiṉum nilakkiḻārkaḷiṉ ottuḻippu illiyāyiṉum ic caṭṭam niṟivēṟātu. ākavē, paṇṇiyāḷkaḷiṉ nalatti virumpum it tiṭṭattic caṭṭamākkiya perumiyum naṭi muṟiyil koṇarnta perumiyum nilakkiḻārkaḷiyum uṅkaḷiyumē cārum. uṅkaḷ aṉivarukkum .iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ purivāṉāka! eṉ maṉamārnta naṉṟiyiyum vaṇakkattiyum uṅkaḷukkut terivittuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟik kikkūppi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
avaḷ pēccu muṭintatum caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum pārviyāḷarum neṭunēram kitaṭṭi āravāram ceytaṉar. maṅkiyarkkaraci eḻuntu mīṇṭum kikūppi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. appoḻutu ‘ṭūmīl’ eṉṟu tuppākki veṭiyōci kēṭṭatu. tuppākiyiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭṭa kuṇṭu maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ mārpit tuḷittatu. avaḷ kūppiya kikaḷuṭaṉ nāṟkāliyiṉ mītu cāyntāḷ.
caṭṭamaṉṟam paraparappaṭintatu. caṭṭa maṉṟak katavukaḷ uṭaṉē tāḷiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru vāyililum pōlīcār kāval vikkappaṭṭaṉar. pārviyāḷaruḷ oruvaṉāka iruntu cuṭṭavaṉ piṭipaṭṭāṉ. pōlīcār uṭaṉē avaṉit taṉicciṟiyil aṭittu vittaṉar.
amiccarkaḷum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum aṭinta tuyarattiṟku aḷavilli. maṅkiyiṉ uṭal avaḷ vaḷamaṉikku eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. aṅku ellōrum kūṭiviṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭukku nalam viḷitta naṅki oruvaṉāl cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭāḷ. eṉṟa ceyti kāṭṭuttīppōl nakaram muḻutum paraviyatu. veḷiyūrkaḷukkut tantikaḷ paṟantaṉa. ‘avaḷ uṭali maṟunāḷ aṭakkam ceyyuṅkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ vantuviṭuvōm’ eṉṟu pala tantikaḷ mutal amiccarukku vantaṉa. maṅkiyiṉ kaḻuttil māli iṭampeṟṟatu. avaḷatu āviyaṟṟa uṭal vaḷamaṉiyiṉ periya kūṭattil, varupavar eḷitil pārkkumpaṭi vikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ilaṭcakkaṇak kāṉa ceṉṉi nakaramakkaḷ vantu pārttu, avaḷ tiruvaṭikaḷit toṭṭuk kaṇkaḷil oṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.
maṟunāṭ kāli avvaḷamaṉiyiṉ paranta veḷi yiṭattil mēṭi iṭampeṟṟatu. ammēṭiyil avaḷatu uṭal kiṭattap peṟṟatu. veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntu vanta perumakkaḷ ovvoruvarākac ceṉṟu avaḷukkut taṅkaḷ iṟuti vaṇakkattit terivittuk koṇṭaṉar. innikaḻccikkup piṟaku iraṅkal kūṭṭam naṭipeṟṟatu. mutal amiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi kūṭṭattiṟkut talimi tāṅkiṉār.
avar eḻuntu niṉṟār; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiṉār. avarāl pēcavē muṭiyavilli. etirkkaṭcit talivar aḻutukoṇṭē avaḷatu peruntoṇṭi naṉku viḷakkiyum pārāṭṭiyum pēciṉār. piṉpu ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum oruvar vītam- avaḷ toṇṭu kaḷiyum paṇpukaḷiyum pārāṭṭip pēciṉar.
avaḷatu uṭal tiruppōrūr aṟaniliyattil aṭakkam ceyyappaṭum eṉpatu aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. aṉṟu māli tiruppōrūril eḷ iṭa iṭamilli. pallāyirakkaṇakkil makkaḷ kūṭiviṭṭaṉar. vaḻipāṭṭu maṇṭapattiṟku etiril irunta ciṟiya pūṅkāviṉ naṭuvil maṅkiyiṉ uṭal aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu. avvamayam, īṉṟa tāyi iḻanta kuḻantikaḷ pōla amiccar mutal āṇṭi īṟāka irunta aṉivarum kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiṉar. appoḻutu muttappar ellōriyum kikūppi vaṇaṅkiṉār; “perumakkaḷē, eṉṉip potu makkaḷ nalattiṟkāka uḻikkumpaṭi tūṇṭiṉavaḷ maṅkiyarkkaracitāṉ. ulakattār pārviyil avaḷ eṉakku makaḷē tavira, uṇmiyil eṉṉiyum ēḻikaḷiyum vāḻvikka vanta teyvam eṉṟē nāṉ avaḷi matikkiṉṟēṉ. avaḷ kollappaṭṭatu paṟṟi nāṉ varuntavilli. avaḷ virumpiya tiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟiya piṟaku kollappaṭṭāḷ eṉpatu eṉakku āṟutalit tarukiṉṟatu. amerikkāvil āprakām liṅkaṉ aṭimi nīkkac caṭṭam niṟivēṟṟiya piṟakutāṉ oru nāṭaka maṉṟattil cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭār. āṉāl maṅkiyarkkaraciyō, caṭṭam niṟivēṟiyavuṭaṉ- perumakkaḷ pārāṭṭutalukkiṭiyē- caṭṭa maṉṟattilēyē cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭāḷ.
avaḷic cuṭṭuk koṉṟavar, avaḷatu tiṭṭatti itukāṟum niṟivēṟṟa maṟutta oru paṇṇiyār eṉṟu pōlīs kūṟukiṟatu. avaḷ taṉ tiṭṭattiṟkākat taṉ uyirip pali koṭuttāḷ. iṉiyāvatu eñciya paṇṇiyārkaḷ caṭṭamāṉa tiṭṭattic ceyaṟpaṭuttumpaṭi vēṇṭik koḷḷukiṟēṉ. maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ uyarnta karuttukkaḷ nāṭṭil paravavēṇṭum- camutāyattiṟku nalam viḷivikka vēṇṭum- nāṭṭil vaṟumi nīṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatuvē eṉ āval. nām aṉivarum ittuṟiyil muyaṉṟu iṭiviṭātu pāṭupaṭutal oṉṟē, avaḷatu āṉmāvukku amitiyit tarum” eṉṟu amitiyākap pēciṉār.
mutal amiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi, “maṅkiyarkkaraci vāḻka! avaratu aṟacceyal vāḻka!” eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ellōrum avar coṉṉapaṭiyē urattuk kūṟi vāḻttiṉar.
aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ, “periyōrkaḷē, ēṟattāḻa āyirattu muṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ maturiyil pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉiviyāka oru maṅkiyarkkaraci tōṉṟiṉāḷ. avaḷ muyaṟciyāl pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil civam taḻittatu. namatu maṅkiyarkkaraciyāl piccikkārar kuṭimakkaḷāka māṟiṉar; paṇṇiyāṭkaḷ vāḻkkiyil nalam peṟṟaṉar; anātip piḷḷikaḷ kuṭimakkaḷāyiṉar; anātip peṇkaḷum kimpeṇkaḷum māṉattōṭu piḻikkum vaḻi kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. anta maṅkiyarkkaraciyip periya purāṇam pāṭiya cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, `maṅkiyarkkut taṉiyaraci- eṅkaḷ teyvam’ eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttiṉār. ceyaṟkariya ceyta namatu perumāṭṭiyiyum nām `maṅkiyarkkut taṉiyaraci- –eṅkaḷ teyvam’ eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttuvōmāka! appeṇṇaraci viṭṭuc ceṉṟa aṟappaṇikaḷi nām muḻu maṉattuṭaṉ ceytu veṟṟi kāṇpōmāka!” eṉṟu pēcik kikuvittu vaṇaṅkiṉār.
uṭaṉē kūṭṭattiṉar, “maṅkiyarkkut taṉiyaraci vāḻka”, “eṅkaḷ teyvam vāḻka!” eṉṟu vāḻttoliyi eḻuppiṉar.

mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷ kaṭantaṉa. caṭṭamaṉṟattil uḷḷa uyarnta mēṭiyil maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ uruvaccili iṭam peṟṟatu. atupōṉṟa aḻakiya uruvaccili avaḷatu uṭalip putitta iṭattilum niṟuttappeṟṟatu: vaḻipāṭṭu maṇṭapattilum oṉṟu iṭam peṟṟatu; perumpālāṉa paṇṇiyār māḷikikaḷilum avaḷ uruvappaṭaṅkaḷ iṭampeṟṟaṉa. ivi aṉittiṟkum mēlāka, ēḻimakkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷil avaḷ niṉivu iṭampeṟṟatu.
aṭutta tērtalil maṇivaṇṇaṉ makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭāṉ. vaṇaṅkāmuṭi mīṇṭum mutal amiccar āṉār. maṅki tāṅkiya amiccar patavi maṇivaṇṇaṉukku vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu.

maṅki cuṭṭukkollappaṭṭatu koṭiyavar maṉattiyum kuluṅkac ceytatu. avaḷic cuṭṭuk koṉṟa paṇṇiyār oru kuṭikāraṉ- kūttikkaḷḷaṉ- koṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu ūr makkaḷāl veṟukkappaṭṭavaṉ; putiya tiṭṭam ceyaṟpaṭumāyiṉ tāṉ atukāṟum nukarntu vanta cukapōkaṅkaḷ keṭṭuviṭumē eṉṟa kavaliyāl avaḷic cuṭṭukkollat tuṇintāṉ. caṭ.ṭam niṟivēṟṟappaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē caṭṭamaṉṟattil avaḷik kolla niṉintu avaṉ pārviyār iṭattil amarntiruntāṉ. nāṭṭiṉ nallakālam avaṉ cuṭuvataṟku muṉ caṭṭam niṟivēṟiviṭṭatu. avaṉ taṉ kuṟṟatti oppukkoṇṭāṉ. avaṉukkut tūkkut taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu.
atuvariyil maṅkiyiṉ tiṭṭatti ēṟkātirunta piṟar avaḷ iṟappukkup piṉ maṉamāṟṟam peṟṟaṉar; tāṅkaḷ attiṭṭatti ēṟṟukkoṇṭatāka mutal amiccarukku aṟivittaṉar.

maṇivaṇṇaṉ amiccaṉākip pēca eḻunta mutal caṭṭa maṉṟak kūṭṭattilēyē itaṉi aṟivittāṉ: “periyōrkaḷē, eṉ āruyir maṉivi maṅkiyarkkaraciyiṉ tiṭṭam caṭṭamāṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, iṉṟu ellōrālum ceyaṟpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉpati makiḻcciyōṭu terivittukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. itē tiṭṭattip piṉpaṟṟi iṉi mutalāḷi- toḻilāḷiyiṉ tiṭṭattit tayārikka vēṇṭum. attiṭṭatti etirkkaṭcit talivarōṭu kalantu uruvākki āṟu mātaṅkaḷuḷ uṅkaḷ muṉpu vippēṉ. ataṟku uṅkaḷ ātaravu tēvi” eṉṟu kūṟik kikkuvittu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ.
avaṉatu putiya tiṭṭattip pārāṭṭuvataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka uṟuppiṉar aṉivarum caṭṭamaṉṟamē atirumpaṭi kitaṭṭi āravārittaṉar.
maṉitap paṇpu vaḷarka!
